[主驾] 主驾座椅靠背角度后挪三档 → seat_angle(direction=back, seatPart=backrest, position=driver, value=3, unit=level)
[主驾] 右侧后排车窗高度调到三分之一 → window_height(function=set, position=backRight, value=1/3)
[主驾] HUD高度调大点 → hud_height(function=increase, value=little)
[主驾] 二排坐垫通风调低点 → seat_ventilation_gear(function=decrease, position=secondRow, value=little, seatPart=cushion)
[主驾] 副驾坐垫加热设小1档 → seat_heater_gear(function=decrease, position=copilot, value=1, unit=level, seatPart=cushion)
[主驾] 副驾舒云座椅腿托调到最上 → seat_vertical_position(function=set, seatPart=legRest, seatType=舒享座椅, position=copilot, value=maxUp)
[主驾] 主驾坐垫通风调大三档 → seat_ventilation_gear(function=increase, position=driver, value=3, unit=level, seatPart=cushion)
[主驾] 帮我开大前排窗户三分之一呀 → window_opener(function=increase, position=front, value=1/3)
[主驾] 二排左座椅座垫调到最高 → seat_vertical_position(function=set, seatPart=cushion, position=secondRowLeft, value=max)
[主驾] 主司机车窗打开1/3 → window_opener(function=set, position=driver, value=1/3)
